Let's think about using `np.convolve` in internal score? For n=16k, FFT score faster. Need exact cmax of linear conv. Implement:
```
def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=1<<(2*n-1).bit_length()
    A=fft(a,size)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
Due to floating errors, c can have tiny negative at ends; max okay. For very large cmax target ~1.5, noise negligible.

Projection:
Sort O(n log n). Need maybe many calls. Could implement faster for nonnegative target sum S: if v sum close and min>=0, just scale. Many iterates after projection remain nonnegative; we can do:
```
if v.min() >=0 and abs(v.sum()-S)<1e-9*S:
   return v
if v.min() >= -1e-12:
   v=np.maximum(v,0); return v*S/v.sum()
```
Then full projection if many negatives. This saves sort. In FISTA, y may have negatives due acceleration, but x projected. For x_new before projection, many negatives? maybe. We can still use fast path.

Project simplex algorithm careful. For S target, if S=0 return zeros. Use w=v-cons? Standard:
```
def proj(v,S):
  n=len(v)
  v=np.asarray(v,float)
  w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
  cs=np.cumsum(w)
  rng=np.arange(1,n+1)
  cond = w - (cs-S)/rng > 0
  rho=cond[:-1].nonzero()[0][-1]+1
  theta=(cs[rho-1]-S)/rho
  return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Actually valid if theta < w_i. Standard. If no, all zeros? Use fallback.

Alternatively use `np.max` lambda? no.

FFT caching: For same n, size constant. We can pass size.

LSE gradient and F:
```
def lse_objective(c, beta):
    cmax=c.max()
    # to avoid underflow, shift by cmax
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    lse=np.log(np.exp(z).sum()) # maybe stable with logsumexp
    return cmax + lse/beta
```
We need gradient weights:
```
z=beta*(c-cmax)
z-=z.max() (which 0)
e=np.exp(z)
w=e/e.sum()
```
If c large (1.5), beta up to 2000, exp(0) fine, negative underflow okay. Use np.exp.

Gradient:
```
def lse_grad(a, c, beta, size):
    cmax=c.max()
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    e=np.exp(z - z.max())
    w=e/e.sum()
    W=fft(w, size)
    A=fft(a, size)
    g=2*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:len(a)]
    return g
```
But w over full 2n-1 weights sum 1. The derivative of cmax+(1/β)LSE is same w. Good.

```
def fista_lse_p(a0, time_budget, beta_start, beta_end, lr_start, warm):
  a=project(a0,S); y=a.copy(); t=1
  # use backtracking for step
  c = conv(y); F = lse(c,beta)
  while time < budget:
    beta=...
    c=conv(y); g=grad
    # estimate step: Lip? Use BB: previous gradient/diff. Or fixed step * beta?
    step = lr * beta? maybe start small.
    x=proj(y - step*g/S? scale)
    c_x=conv(x); F_x=...
    if F_x <= F + 1e-8: accept, else reduce step a few times.
    t_new=...
    y=x + (t-1)/t_new*(x-a)
    y=np.maximum(y,0?) not enough sum; project? For FISTA, extrapolate y should be in domain? Many implementations project y? If not, conv(y) may have sum not S and negatives; but we can clip/project y each iteration to domain before evaluating: y=project(y,S). This loses acceleration? For nonconvex, maybe project y to avoid bad. We can do y=project(y,S) to keep valid. This is heavy but okay.
```

```
def adam_lse_p(a0,time,beta,lr):
  m,v=zero
  a=project(a0,S)
  for step:
    c=conv(a); g=grad
    m=β1m+(1-β1)g; v=...
    a_new=project(a - lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps), S)
```

```
def adam_lse_u(a0,time,beta,lr):
  p=project(a0,S)
  u=np.log(p) - mean(np.log(p)) # gauge
  m,v=zeros
  best=p
  for:
    p=exp(u)/Z (ensure p sum S? If exp sum not S? We can scale p to S after normalization; gauge remove mean)
    c=conv(p); g=grad
    grad_u = g - p*(np.dot(p,g))
    # remove mean for gauge? grad_u sum=0 if p sum1; if sum S, dot? Formula for p=Z exp(u), ∂p/∂u_i = p_i(δ - p_i/S). gradient_u = g_p (δ - p_i/S). For p sum S, p_i/S is normalized. So grad_u = g - p*(g·(p/S))/S? Let's derive: p_i = S e^{u_i}/Z. ∂p_j/∂u_i = S e^{u_i}/Z (δ_{ij} - e^{u_j}/Z) = p_i(δ - p_j/S). Gradient objective with respect p: g_j (=∂F/∂p? But F homogeneous scale? if sum fixed, but using p variation, need constraint). Since gauge, define P=p/S in simplex. F depends P. g_P = ∂F/∂P. Then ∂F/∂u_i = ∑_j g_P_j/p_j? Wait P_j=p_j/S. If p= S P, F(p)=F(S P) = F(P) if F homogeneous? score cmax? c scales S², F cmax scales S², not scale invariant if we don't fix sum. We fix S constant. If vary u, p sum S. Let F(S P). ∂F/∂P = S ∂F/∂p? If F homogeneous degree2 cmax, ∂wrt P = S g_p? But anyway. Use normalized q=p/S sum1. Compute gradient w.r.t q: G = grad_p (same because transform q). Then ∂F/∂u_i = S? Let's simplify use parameter q=sum1, q_i=e^{u}/Z. ∂q_j/∂u_i = q_i(δ_{ij}-q_j). Use G_j=∂F/∂q. grad_u_i=∑ G_j q_i(δ - q_j)=q_i (G_i - ∑G q). Then p=S q, c scales S². We can operate on q with S=1 for smooth objective, and final scale. But c values ~1/n tiny if sum1. Better scale: Use q sum1, objective F_q = 2n cmax_q (score). Gradient w.r.t q = 2n grad_c? Actually F_p(p)=F_q(p/S). ∂F_p/∂p_i = (1/S)∂F_q/∂q_i. We compute grad_c for p as per evaluate? If using F_cmax_p (with p sum S) derivative wrt p = 2 w shift p. F_q = cmax_q? cmax_q = cmax_p/S². - `internal_score(a)`:
```
```
def internal_score(a):
  if len(a)<1 or not finite return inf
  n=len(a)
  s=float(a.sum())
  if s<0.01: return inf
  # if size not two large? use fft
  size = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
  A=fft(a,size)
  c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
  cmax=float(np.max(c))
  return float(2*n*cmax/(s*s))
```
Since S may not be √(2n) if a not normalized. For optimized we keep S.
- `normalize_to_sum(a,S)`: clip nonnegative, if sum>0 scale, else ones.
- `make_p_from_u(u,S)`: stable softmax.

No overflow.

```
def dense_u_run(a0, n, time_budget, beta, lr, rng, label, best_score=None):
    S = sqrt(2n)
    N = next_pow2(2n-1)
    p = normalize_to_sum(a0,S)
    u = log(p/S); u -= mean; clip
    # compute cmax,F
    A,c,cmax,F = eval_u(u)
    if cmax < best_score: best...
    m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0; scale=1; global_lr=lr
    no_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time<start+budget:
       it+=1
       # schedule lr? maybe global_lr = lr * max(0.05, 1/(1+0.005*it/1000))? We can decay slowly.
       # Compute grad using current c,A (we have). If we recomputed at start of each loop, ensure c/F correspond to current u.
       z = beta*(c-cmax) ... weights; F computed
       g=2*ifft(...); grad_u...
       # if grad norm tiny break?
       # Try line search
       accepted=False
       for ls in range(4):
          step = global_lr*scale
          # Adam state update trial? Use m,v update based on grad_u and t+1
          m_trial = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad_u
          v_trial = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad_u*grad_u
          bias1=1-beta1**(t+1); bias2=1-beta2**(t+1)
          u_trial = u - step * (m_trial/bias1)/(sqrt(v_trial/bias2)+1e-8)
          u_trial -= mean; clip
          p_trial,c_trial,max_trial,F_trial = eval_u_from_p? We need compute p_trial/c. eval u does FFT.
          if F_trial <= F + 1e-9*max(1,F) or (c_trial < cmax - 1e-7): accept
          else scale*=0.5
       if accepted:
          update u,m,v,t; c,A,F=c_trial...; if cmax improved no_improve=0 else no_improve+=1
       else:
          global_lr *=0.5
          if global_lr < 1e-5: break? or reset with noise
          # maybe recompute grad? keep.
       # If no_improve > 5000 and rng random: perturb u from best_u plus noise, reset m,v, scale=1
    return best
```

```
def next_pow2(m):
```

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    n=a.size
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=fft(a,size)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0*n*cmax/(s*s))
```
For optimized arrays sum S; score cmax.

`normalize_sum(a,S)`:
```
def normalize_sum(a,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,np.float64)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s <=1e-300:
      return np.full(len(a), S/len(a))
   return a*(S/s)
```
But if len 0? handle.

`resample_to_length(a,n)`:
```
def resample_to_length(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
   if a.size==n: return a.copy()
   if a.size==0: return np.ones(n)
   if a.size==1: return np.full(n,a[0])
   xo=np.linspace(0,1,a.size,endpoint=False)
   xi=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   # if a has large values, linear interpolation
   return np.interp(xi,xo,a)
```
For downsample >n, np.interp takes points not averaging; okay.

`u_to_p(u,S)`:
```
def u_to_p(u,S):
   u=np.asarray(u,float)
   u-=u.max() # stable
   e=np.exp(u)
   s=e.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return ones
   p=S*e/s
   return p
```
But subtracting max may lose center; okay. If u has large spread, e of negative underflow, fine.


`lse_weights(c,cmax,beta)`:
```
def lse_weights(c,cmax,beta):
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    # z max 0; may have tiny positive due cmax? ensure
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,-1000.0)
    # if beta huge, okay
    e=np.exp(z)
    se=e.sum()
    if se<=0: # all underflow? set weights at argmax c
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w
    return e/se
```
F:
```
def lse_value(c,cmax,beta):
    z=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(z); se=e.sum(); return cmax+math.log(max(se,1e-300))/beta
```
We can compute both in one to avoid duplicate in current. For line search trials, we need F; can recompute exp. Overhead okay.

But in dense_u_run, for each trial current we compute `z` for F. For current gradient we need weights. We can compute once for current. For trial, compute F only. A/c trial already. Implement separate.

To save exp in trial, we can compute F using same z. That's okay.

Gradient function:
```
def lse_gradient(p,A,c,w,size,n):
   W=fft(w,size)
   g=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
   return g
```
Need w length 2n-1. If w nan? ensure.

`u_gradient(grad_p,p,S)`:
```
def u_gradient(grad_p,p,S):
   # grad_p derivative wrt p (sum S)
   mean_term=float(np.dot(p, grad_p)/S)
   return p*(grad_p - mean_term)
```
If S=0 no.

`clip_center_u(u, u_max=30.0)`:
```
def clip_center_u(u,u_max=30):
    u=np.asarray(u,float)
    u-=np.mean(u)
    np.clip(u,-u_max,u_max,out=u)
    # after clip mean may shift; center again?
    u-=np.mean(u)
    return u
```
Clipping after center can change mean. To keep zero mean, maybe center after clip. We'll do center after clip; then some values may slightly exceed due mean shift negligible. Or clip then center no clip.

```
def dense_u_run(a0, n, max_time, beta, lr, rng, label='', verbose=True):
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max_time
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    p=normalize_sum(a0,S)
    u=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)/S)
    u=clip_center_u(u)
    p,c,cmax,A=eval_u(...)
    if verbose print start
    best_cmax=cmax; best_p=p.copy(); best_u=u.copy()
    F=_lse_value(c,cmax,beta)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; scale=1.0; global_lr=lr
    no_improve=0; rej_streak=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    min_lr=1e-5
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if it % 2000 ==0: print timing and cmax
       # compute weights/F? We have F current. Need weights for grad.
       w=lse_weights(c,cmax,beta)
       g=...
       gu=u_gradient(g,p,S)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(gu)
       if not finite or gn<1e-18: break
       accepted=False
       best_trial=None; best_trial_F=inf; best_trial_c=inf
       for ls in range(5):
          step=global_lr*scale
          t1=t+1
          m1=0.9*m+0.1*gu
          v1=0.999*v+0.001*gu*gu
          b1=1-0.9**t1; b2=1-0.999**t1
          upd=(m1/b1)/(np.sqrt(v1/b2)+1e-8)
          u_t=u - step*upd
          u_t=clip_center_u(u_t)
          p_t,c_t,cmax_t,A_t=eval_u(...)
          F_t=_lse_value(c_t,cmax_t,beta)
          if F_t < best_trial_F: store
          if (F_t <= F + 1e-9*max(1,abs(F)) or cmax_t < cmax - 1e-10): accept
          else scale*=0.5
       if accepted:
          u=u_t; p=p_t; c=c_t; cmax=cmax_t; A=A_t; F=F_t; m=m1; v=v1; t=t1
          if cmax < best_cmax: best... no_improve=0 else no_improve+=1
          scale=1.0; rej_streak=0
       else:
          # accept best trial if it is lower in cmax, or reset lr
          if best_trial is not None and best_trial_c < cmax:
             accept best_trial (update m/v? use last? maybe set t+1, m/v from last) ; best_trial_F may >F but accept to progress
             no_improve maybe if cmax not best
          else:
             global_lr=max(min_lr, global_lr*0.5)
             if global_lr <= min_lr+1e-12:
                # small perturbation
                u=clip_center_u(best_u + rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
                p,c,cmax,A=eval_u(...)
                F=lse_value(...)
                m=zeros;v=zeros;t=0;scale=1;global_lr=lr
                # do not accept? current set
             else:
                # maybe re-use same; to avoid infinite, perturb tiny
                if rej_streak>3:
                   u=clip_center_u(u + rng.normal(0,0.01,n)); p,c,cmax,A=eval_u; F=...
                   m=zeros...
                rej_streak+=1
       if no_improve > 30000:
          # restart from best with noise
          noise_level=0.02+0.05*(beta>100)
          u=clip_center_u(best_u + rng.normal(0,noise_level,n))
          p,c,cmax,A=eval_u(...)
          F=lse_value(...)
          m=zeros;v=zeros;t=0;scale=1;no_improve=0
       # If cmax below very low maybe continue? global best handled outside
    return best_p, best_cmax
```

Generation functions:
```
def random_fourier_u(n, rng, max_freq=12, max_amp=0.8):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    u=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1,max_freq+1):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.0, max_amp/math.sqrt(m)) # or 0.3
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
       u += amp*math.cos(2*math.pi*m*t+phase)
       u += amp*math.sin(2*math.pi*m*t+phase) # two dims random
    return u
```
This u zero mean-ish. p=softmax(u)*S. Use as initial u? Our optimizer takes p; can convert. Or generate p directly: p=exp(clip(u)) normalized. Fine.
Base `make_from_u(u,S)` = u_to_p. Could use u with high frequency to get shape. Use `u_to_p`.

But optimizer initial `a0` p; it takes log. If p generated from u, fine.

Initializations with u:
- Random Fourier 3 variants.
- Random exponential: t, for a in [-2,-1,1,2,4]: u=a*(t-0.5) maybe; p=softmax.
- Smooth asymmetric polynomial: u = a*(t-0.5)+b*(t-0.5)^2.

Let's flesh each.

`eval_p(p,size)`:
```
def eval_p(p,size):
    n=len(p)
    A=fft(p,size)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # clean extremely tiny neg? max unaffected.
    cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return c,cmax,A
```
Need avoid ifft returning tiny imaginary; real.

`eval_u`:
```
def eval_u(u,S,size,n):
    p=u_to_p(u,S)
    c,cmax,A=eval_p(p,size)
    return p,c,cmax,A
```

`lse_value`: To avoid math.log of 0, use np.log.
```
def lse_value(c,cmax,beta):
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    # z max <=0, but due fp could >0; subtract max
    zm=float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm): return cmax
    e=np.exp(z-zm)
    se=float(e.sum())
    if se <=0: return cmax
    return float(cmax + (zm + math.log(se))/beta)
```
Since we need F = cmax + (1/β)log∑exp(β(c-cmax)). If z shift by zm, log∑=zm+log∑e. Equivalent. If cmax is max, zm ≈0. Good.

`lse_weights`:
```
def lse_weights(c,cmax,beta):
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    zm=float(np.max(z))
    if not finite: weights top
    e=np.exp(z-zm)
    se=e.sum()
    if se<=0:
       w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[np.argmax(c)]=1; return w
    w=e/se
    # nan to zero
    w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0)
    s=w.sum(); if s<=0: ... else w/=s
    return w
```

`gradient_p`:
```
def gradient_p(w,p,A,size,n):
    W=fft(w,size)
    g=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
    return g
```

`gradient_u`:
```
def gradient_u(g,p,S):
    mean_term=float(np.dot(p,g)/S)
    gu=p*(g-mean_term)
    gu=np.where(np.isfinite(gu),gu,0)
    return gu
```

`center_clip_u`:
```
def center_clip_u(u,u_max=24.0):
    u=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    u -= float(np.mean(u))
    u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)
    u -= float(np.mean(u))
    return u
```
Clipping then center might make some entries slightly >u_max; not issue. To guarantee, could iterate but no.

```
def init_blocks(n,S,K, rng=None, mode='sin'):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    h=np.ones(K)
    if mode=='sin':
       f=rng.uniform(0.5,2.5); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); amp=rng.uniform(0.3,0.8)
       h = 1 + amp*np.sin(2π*f*np.arange(K)/K + ph)
    else:
       h = rng.random(K) + 0.2
    # ensure positive
    h=np.maximum(h,0.05)
    seg=[n//K + (1 if i<n%K else 0) for i in range(K)] no lambda? use loop.
    p=np.empty(n)
    for i,hval in enumerate(h): fill
    return normalize_sum(p,S)
```

```
def dense_u_run(a0, n, max_time, beta, lr, rng, label=''):
    if max_time <= 0:
       return normalize_sum(a0, math.sqrt(2*n)), math.inf
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    p=normalize_sum(a0,S)
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
    u=np.log(p/S)
    u=center_clip_u(u,24.0)
    p,c,cmax,A=eval_u(u,S,size,n)
    best_c=cmax; best_p=p.copy(); best_u=u.copy()
    F=lse_value(c,cmax,beta)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    t=0; global_lr=float(lr); scale=1.0
    no_improve=0; rej_iters=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    print(...)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       if not finite all break
       if it % 5000 ==0: print
       w=lse_weights(c,cmax,beta)
       g=gradient_p(w,p,A,size,n)
       gu=gradient_u(g,p,S)
       gn=float(np.linalg.norm(gu))
       if not finite or gn < 1e-16:
           break
       # optional scaling if gn very large? not.
       accepted=False
       best_attempt=None
       for ls in range(4):
          step=global_lr*scale
          if step <=0: break
          t1=t+1
          m1=0.9*m +0.1*gu
          v1=0.999*v +0.001*gu*gu
          b1=1.0 - 0.9**t1
          b2=1.0 - 0.999**t1
          upd=(m1/b1)/(np.sqrt(v1/b2)+1e-8)
          ut=u - step*upd
          ut=center_clip_u(ut,24.0)
          pt,ct,cmax_t,At=eval_u(ut,S,size,n)
          Ft=lse_value(ct,cmax_t,beta)
          if best_attempt is None or Ft < best_attempt[4] or cmax_t < best_attempt[2]: store (ut,pt,ct,cmax_t,At,Ft,m1,v1,t1,upd?)
          if np.isfinite(Ft) and (Ft <= F + 1e-8*max(1.0,abs(F)) or cmax_t < cmax - 1e-10):
              # accept
              accepted=True
              ut,pt,ct,cmax_t,At,Ft,m1,v1,t1 = store
              break
          scale *=0.5
       if accepted:
          u,p,c,cmax,A,F,m,v,t = ut,pt,ct,cmax_t,At,Ft,m1,v1,t1
          if cmax < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=cmax; best_p=p.copy(); best_u=u.copy(); no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve +=1
          scale=1.0; rej_iters=0
       else:
          if best_attempt is not None:
             # accept best attempt if it improves cmax or reduces F significantly (allow small increase)
             ut,pt,ct,cmax_t,At,Ft,m1,v1,t1 = best_attempt
             if cmax_t < cmax - 1e-9 or Ft < F - 1e-8:
                u,p,c,cmax,A,F,m,v,t = ut,pt,ct,cmax_t,At,Ft,m1,v1,t1
                if cmax < best_c - 1e-12: best... else no_improve+=1
                scale=1.0; rej_iters=0
             else:
                rej_iters +=1
                global_lr = 0.5*global_lr
                if global_lr < 1e-5:
                   # restart from best or small noise
                   if rej_iters > 4:
                       u = center_clip_u(best_u + rng.normal(0,0.05,n),24.0)
                       p,c,cmax,A=eval_u(u,S,size,n)
                       F=lse_value(c,cmax,beta)
                       m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; scale=1.0; global_lr=float(lr); no_improve=0
                       continue
          # if not accepted, continue; loop will recompute same grad? To avoid infinite, if rej_iters>2 perturb small
          if rej_iters >= 3:
              u = center_clip_u(u + rng.normal(0,0.01,n),24.0)
              p,c,cmax,A=eval_u(u,S,size,n)
              F=lse_value(c,cmax,beta)
              m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; scale=1.0
              rej_iters=0
       if no_improve > 10000:
          noise_std = 0.02 if beta < 100 else 0.01
          u = center_clip_u(best_u + rng.normal(0,noise_std,n),24.0)
          p,c,cmax,A=eval_u(u,S,size,n)
          F=lse_value(c,cmax,beta)
          m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; scale=1.0; no_improve=0
    # after loop, ensure best_p sum S
    best_p=normalize_sum(best_p,S)
    return best_p,best_c
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+max(0,budget-60)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # choose n
    if budget >= 2000: n=32768
    elif budget >=1200: n=16384
    else: n=8192
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    # prev
    prev_a=None
    try:
       prev_raw=height_sequence_1
       if prev_raw is not None:
          prev_arr=np.asarray(prev_raw,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
          prev_arr=np.where(np.isfinite(prev_arr),prev_arr,0.0)
          prev_arr=np.clip(prev_arr,0,1000)
          if prev_arr.size>0 and prev_arr.sum()>0:
             prev_a=prev_arr
    except Exception:
       prev_a=None
    # maybe use should match n? We'll resample.
    inits=make_initializations(n,S,rng,prev_a)
    scored=[]
    for p in inits:
       p=normalize_sum(p,S)
       sc=internal_score(p)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,p))
    scored.sort(key=lambda? cannot lambda? Rule no lambda. Use `scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])`? No lambda functions. Need avoid lambda. Use list of tuples and `scored.sort()` because tuple first score. Good.
    best_a=None; best_score=np.inf
    if scored:
       best_score=scored[0][0]; best_a=scored[0][1].copy()
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.full(n,S/n); best_score=2.0
    print initial best.
    top_count=3
    # coarse
    for idx in range(min(top_count,len(scored))):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60: break
       # allocate ~100s each or remaining / top
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       # if idx 0 maybe 120, else 80
       alloc = min(120.0, rem/(top_count-idx))
       if alloc > 5:
           a_i, sc_i = dense_u_run(scored[idx][1], n, alloc, 20.0, 0.2, rng, label='coarse%d'%idx)
           if sc_i < best_score: best_score=sc_i; best_a=a_i.copy(); print
    # medium
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem>60:
       med=min(600.0, rem*0.3)
       a_m, sc_m = dense_u_run(best_a,n,med,80.0,0.08,rng,'medium')
       update
    # fine
    rem=...
    if rem>60:
       fine=min(1000.0, rem*0.5)
       a_f, sc_f = dense_u_run(best_a,n,fine,250.0,0.03,rng,'fine')
       update
    # micro
    rem=...
    if rem>30:
       micro=min(300.0, rem*0.5)
       a_mi, sc_mi = dense_u_run(best_a,n,micro,800.0,0.01,rng,'micro')
       update
```

`make_initializations` top-level:
```
def make_initializations(n,S,rng,prev_a):
    out=[]
    base=np.full(n,S/n)
    out.append(base.copy())
    if prev_a is not None and len(prev_a)>0:
        out.append(resample_to_length(prev_a,n))
    # Fourier
    for mf,amp in [(8,0.6),(16,0.45),(48,0.35)]:
        out.append(init_fourier(n,S,rng,mf,amp))
    # exp
    for rate in [-4,-2,-1,1,2,4,6]:
        out.append(init_exp(n,S,rate))
    # RBF
    for nb in [4,8,12]:
       out.append(init_rbf(n,S,rng,nb))
    # blocks
    for K in [2,4,8,16,32]:
       out.append(init_blocks(n,S,K, rng, 0))
       out.append(init_blocks(n,S,K, rng, 1))
    return out
```

For K=2 special heights: in `init_blocks`, if K==2 and mode==0, use h=[2/3,4/3]; if mode==1 maybe reverse [4/3,2/3]. Since chain norm. For K>2 random.
Implement `init_blocks(n,S,K,rng,mode)`:
```
h=np.ones(K)
if K==2:
   h=np.array([2.0/3.0, 4.0/3.0]) if mode==0 else reversed
else:
   if mode==0:
      f=0.5+1.5*rng.random(); ph=2πr; amp=0.5+0.4*rng.random()
      hh=np.arange(K)/K
      h=1+amp*np.sin(2π f hh+ph)
   else:
      h=rng.random(K)+0.2
   h=np.maximum(h,0.05)
p=np.empty(n)
seg=n//K; rem=n%K; pos=0
for i in range(K):
   L=seg+(1 if i<rem else 0)
   p[pos:pos+L]=h[i]
   pos+=L
return normalize_sum(p,S)
```

```python
"""
Non-negative sequence search for
    score = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The method uses a smooth log-sum-exp surrogate of the peak convolution
coefficient, optimizes logarithmic/softmax variables (which hard-keep
non-negativity and normalization), and uses several global initializations:
previous construction if available, Fourier/polynomial/RBF/block starts.
Line search and periodic restarts are used to keep the nonconvex descent stable.
The length is chosen adaptively (mainly 8192/16384) to balance quality and speed.
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sum(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64) * max(0.0, float(S))
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, float(S) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(S) / s)


def resample_to_length(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=False)
    xi = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(xi, xo, a)
    return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)


def internal_score(a):
    """
    FFT-based value of the objective, matching np.convolve(a, a)
    for the nonnegatve/clipped sequence.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    size = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = fft(a, size)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:2 * n - 1]
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def eval_p(p, size):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    A = fft(p, size)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:2 * n - 1]
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return c, cmax, A


def u_to_p(u, S):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        u = u - float(np.max(u))
        e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(u.size, float(S) / float(u.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return float(S) * (e / s)


def eval_u(u, S, size, n):
    p = u_to_p(u, S)
    c, cmax, A = eval_p(p, size)
    return p, c, cmax, A


def lse_value(c, cmax, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        z = float(beta) * (c - float(cmax))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -1000.0)
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return float(cmax)
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        e = np.exp(z - zm)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 1e-300:
        return float(cmax)
    return float(cmax + (zm + math.log(se)) / float(beta))


def lse_weights(c, cmax, beta, mix=0.0):
    """
    Weights for the smooth max. A small uniform mix is intentionally included:
    it avoids zero-gradient situations for a single symmetric active lag and
    keeps pressure on near-active coefficients.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    M = int(c.size)
    if M == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        z = float(beta) * (c - float(cmax))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -1000.0)
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        e = np.exp(z - zm)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / se
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / s

    if mix > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - float(mix)) * w + (float(mix) / float(M))
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s > 1e-300:
            w = w / s
    return w


def gradient_p(w, A, n):
    # sum_k w_k * d c_k / d p_i = 2 sum_k w_k p_{k-i}
    W = fft(w, A.size)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)


def gradient_u(g, p, S):
    # p_i = S softmax(u)_i;  dp_j/du_i = p_i(delta_ij - p_j/S)
    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
        mean_term = float(np.dot(p, g)) / float(S)
    gu = p * (g - mean_term)
    return np.where(np.isfinite(gu), gu, 0.0)


def center_clip_u(u, u_max=24.0):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    u = u - float(np.mean(u))
    u = np.clip(u, -u_max, u_max)
    u = u - float(np.mean(u))
    return u


def init_from_u(n, S, u):
    u = center_clip_u(u, 30.0)
    return u_to_p(u, S)


def init_fourier(n, S, rng, max_freq, amp):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, max_freq + 1):
        scale = amp / math.sqrt(float(m))
        ac = rng.uniform(0.0, scale)
        as_ = rng.uniform(0.0, scale)
        pc = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        ps = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        om = 2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * t
        u = u + ac * np.cos(om + pc) + as_ * np.sin(om + ps)
    return init_from_u(n, S, u)


def init_exp(n, S, rate):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x = t - 0.5
    u = float(rate) * x + 0.35 * float(rate) * (x * x) + 0.12 * float(rate) * (x * x * x)
    return init_from_u(n, S, u)


def init_rbf(n, S, rng, n_bumps):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(int(n_bumps)):
        c = rng.random()
        w = 0.04 + 0.25 * rng.random()
        a = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        d = (t - c) / w
        u = u + a * np.exp(-0.5 * d * d)
    return init_from_u(n, S, u)


def init_blocks(n, S, K, rng, mode):
    K = int(max(2, K))
    h = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 2:
        if mode == 0:
            h[0] = 2.0 / 3.0
            h[1] = 4.0 / 3.0
        else:
            h[0] = 4.0 / 3.0
            h[1] = 2.0 / 3.0
    else:
        if mode == 0:
            f = 0.5 + 2.0 * rng.random()
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
            amp = 0.45 + 0.55 * rng.random()
            x = np.arange(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
            h = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph)
        else:
            h = 0.25 + rng.random(K)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.05)

    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = n // K
    rem = n % K
    pos = 0
    for i in range(K):
        L = base + (1 if i < rem else 0)
        p[pos:pos + L] = float(h[i])
        pos += L
    return normalize_sum(p, S)


def make_initializations(n, S, rng, prev_a):
    out = []
    # Constant
    out.append(np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    # Previous construction, if present
    if prev_a is not None:
        pa = np.asarray(prev_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if pa.size > 0 and float(np.sum(np.clip(pa, 0.0, 1000.0))) > 0.0:
            out.append(resample_to_length(pa, n))

    # Fourier starts
    out.append(init_fourier(n, S, rng, 8, 0.65))
    out.append(init_fourier(n, S, rng, 16, 0.50))
    out.append(init_fourier(n, S, rng, 48, 0.35))

    # Exponential asymmetries
    for r in (-6.0, -3.0, -1.5, 1.5, 3.0, 6.0):
        out.append(init_exp(n, S, r))

    # RBF asymmetric starts
    out.append(init_rbf(n, S, rng, 5))
    out.append(init_rbf(n, S, rng, 10))
    out.append(init_rbf(n, S, rng, 16))

    # Block starts
    for K in (2, 3, 4, 8, 16, 32):
        for mode in (0, 1):
            out.append(init_blocks(n, S, K, rng, mode))

    return out


def dense_u_run(a0, n, max_time, beta, lr, rng, label=""):
    """
    Adam-style descent in softmax/log variables for one fixed smoothing level.
    Returns (best_p, best_cmax_score). For p normalized to sqrt(2n),
    best_cmax_score equals the evaluate_sequence objective.
    """
    n = int(n)
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        p = normalize_sum(a0, S)
        return p, internal_score(p)

    size = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_time)
    start = time.monotonic()

    p = normalize_sum(a0, S)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    u = np.log(p / S)
    u = center_clip_u(u, 24.0)

    p, c, cmax, A = eval_u(u, S, size, n)
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        p = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        c, cmax, A = eval_p(p, size)

    best_cmax = float(cmax)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_u = u.copy()

    mix = 0.05 if float(beta) < 100.0 else 0.10
    F = lse_value(c, cmax, float(beta))

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_adam = 0
    global_lr = float(lr)
    scale = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    reject_runs = 0

    print("[%s] n=%d beta=%.1f lr=%.3f start=%.6f" % (label, n, beta, lr, best_cmax), flush=True)

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if not (np.isfinite(cmax) and np.isfinite(F)):
            u = best_u.copy()
            p, c, cmax, A = eval_u(u, S, size, n)
            F = lse_value(c, cmax, float(beta))
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t_adam = 0
            global_lr = float(lr)
            scale = 1.0
            continue

        if it % 5000 == 0:
            el = time.monotonic() - start
            print("[%s] it=%d cur=%.6f best=%.6f elapsed=%.1fs" % (label, it, cmax, best_cmax, el), flush=True)

        w = lse_weights(c, cmax, float(beta), mix=mix)
        g = gradient_p(w, A, n)
        gu = gradient_u(g, p, S)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(gu)) if gu.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-16:
            break

        accepted = False
        best_attempt = None

        for ls in range(4):
            step = global_lr * scale
            if not np.isfinite(step) or step <= 0.0:
                break
            t1 = t_adam + 1
            m1 = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * gu
            v1 = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (gu * gu)
            b1 = 1.0 - 0.9 ** t1
            b2 = 1.0 - 0.999 ** t1
            upd = (m1 / b1) / (np.sqrt(v1 / b2) + 1e-8)

            ut = u - step * upd
            ut = center_clip_u(ut, 24.0)
            pt, ct, cmax_t, At = eval_u(ut, S, size, n)
            Ft = lse_value(ct, cmax_t, float(beta))

            if (
                (best_attempt is None)
                or (cmax_t < best_attempt[3] - 1e-13)
                or (abs(cmax_t - best_attempt[3]) <= 1e-13 and Ft < best_attempt[5])
            ):
                best_attempt = (ut, pt, ct, cmax_t, At, Ft, m1, v1, t1)

            accept_tol = 1e-9 * max(1.0, abs(F))
            if np.isfinite(Ft) and (Ft <= F + accept_tol and cmax_t <= cmax + 1e-6):
                accepted = True
                ut, pt, ct, cmax_t, At, Ft, m1, v1, t1 = best_attempt
                break
            if np.isfinite(cmax_t) and cmax_t < cmax - 1e-10:
                accepted = True
                ut, pt, ct, cmax_t, At, Ft, m1, v1, t1 = best_attempt
                break

            scale *= 0.5

        if accepted:
            u, p, c, cmax, A, F, m, v, t_adam = ut, pt, ct, cmax_t, At, Ft, m1, v1, t1
            if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-13:
                best_cmax = float(cmax)
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_u = u.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                if it % 1000 == 0:
                    print("[%s] improvement best=%.6f at it=%d" % (label, best_cmax, it), flush=True)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            scale = 1.0
            reject_runs = 0
        else:
            if best_attempt is not None:
                ut, pt, ct, cmax_t, At, Ft, m1, v1, t1 = best_attempt
                if (np.isfinite(cmax_t) and cmax_t < cmax - 1e-9) or (np.isfinite(Ft) and Ft < F - 1e-8):
                    u, p, c, cmax, A, F, m, v, t_adam = ut, pt, ct, cmax_t, At, Ft, m1, v1, t1
                    if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-13:
                        best_cmax = float(cmax)
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        best_u = u.copy()
                        no_improve = 0
                    else:
                        no_improve += 1
                    scale = 1.0
                    reject_runs = 0
                else:
                    reject_runs += 1
                    global_lr *= 0.5
                    if global_lr < 1e-6:
                        if reject_runs >= 4:
                            noise_std = 0.05
                            u = center_clip_u(best_u + rng.normal(0.0, noise_std, n), 24.0)
                            p, c, cmax, A = eval_u(u, S, size, n)
                            F = lse_value(c, cmax, float(beta))
                            m.fill(0.0)
                            v.fill(0.0)
                            t_adam = 0
                            scale = 1.0
                            global_lr = float(lr)
                            reject_runs = 0
                            continue
            if reject_runs >= 2:
                u = center_clip_u(u + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n), 24.0)
                p, c, cmax, A = eval_u(u, S, size, n)
                F = lse_value(c, cmax, float(beta))
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                t_adam = 0
                scale = 1.0
                reject_runs = 0

        if no_improve > 12000:
            noise_std = 0.015 if float(beta) < 100.0 else 0.008
            u = center_clip_u(best_u + rng.normal(0.0, noise_std, n), 24.0)
            p, c, cmax, A = eval_u(u, S, size, n)
            F = lse_value(c, cmax, float(beta))
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t_adam = 0
            scale = 1.0
            no_improve = 0

    best_p = normalize_sum(best_p, S)
    if not np.isfinite(best_cmax):
        best_cmax = internal_score(best_p)
    print("[%s] end best=%.6f" % (label, best_cmax), flush=True)
    return best_p, best_cmax


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    reserve = 45.0
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Main length. 16384 is a good compromise; 8192 for smaller budgets.
    if budget >= 2100.0:
        n_main = 16384
    elif budget >= 1200.0:
        n_main = 8192
    elif budget >= 600.0:
        n_main = 4096
    else:
        n_main = 1024

    S_main = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_main))

    # Optional known previous construction.
    prev_a = None
    try:
        prev_raw = height_sequence_1
        if prev_raw is not None:
            prev_arr = np.asarray(prev_raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            prev_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(prev_arr), prev_arr, 0.0)
            prev_arr = np.clip(prev_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if prev_arr.size > 0 and float(np.sum(prev_arr)) > 0.0:
                prev_a = prev_arr
    except Exception:
        prev_a = None

    global_best_a = None
    global_best_score = float("inf")

    # Keep original previous as a valid fallback candidate at its own length.
    if prev_a is not None:
        try:
            sc_prev = internal_score(prev_a)
            if np.isfinite(sc_prev) and sc_prev < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = float(sc_prev)
                global_best_a = prev_a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Generate and score initializations at the main length.
    inits = make_initializations(n_main, S_main, rng, prev_a)
    scored = []
    for idx, p in enumerate(inits):
        p = normalize_sum(p, S_main)
        if p.size != n_main:
            p = resample_to_length(p, n_main)
        try:
            sc = internal_score(p)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), idx, p))
    scored.sort()

    best_n_a = None
    best_n_score = float("inf")
    if scored:
        best_n_score = float(scored[0][0])
        best_n_a = np.asarray(scored[0][2], dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if best_n_score < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = best_n_score
            global_best_a = best_n_a.copy()
    if best_n_a is None:
        best_n_a = np.full(n_main, S_main / float(n_main), dtype=np.float64)
        best_n_score = internal_score(best_n_a)
        if best_n_score < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = best_n_score
            global_best_a = best_n_a.copy()

    print("initial best n=%d score=%.6f" % (n_main, best_n_score), flush=True)
    if np.isfinite(global_best_score) and global_best_score < best_n_score:
        print("global previous/other best=%.6f" % global_best_score, flush=True)

    # Phase 1: multi-start coarse optimization at the main length.
    top_count = min(3, len(scored)) if scored else 1
    if top_count > 0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0:
            coarse_total = min(rem * 0.18, 360.0)
            each = max(5.0, coarse_total / float(top_count))
            for idx in range(top_count):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                alloc = min(each, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
                if alloc < 5.0:
                    break
                a0, sc0 = dense_u_run(scored[idx][2], n_main, alloc, 20.0, 0.20, rng, "coarse%d" % idx)
                if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_n_score:
                    best_n_score = float(sc0)
                    best_n_a = a0.copy()
                if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < global_best_score:
                    global_best_score = float(sc0)
                    global_best_a = a0.copy()

    # Phase 2: medium polish of the main-length incumbent.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        alloc = min(700.0, max(0.0, rem - 30.0))
        a1, sc1 = dense_u_run(best_n_a, n_main, alloc, 80.0, 0.08, rng, "medium")
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_n_score:
            best_n_score = float(sc1)
            best_n_a = a1.copy()
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(sc1)
            global_best_a = a1.copy()

    # Phase 3: fine polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        alloc = min(1000.0, max(0.0, rem - 30.0))
        a2, sc2 = dense_u_run(best_n_a, n_main, alloc, 250.0, 0.03, rng, "fine")
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_n_score:
            best_n_score = float(sc2)
            best_n_a = a2.copy()
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(sc2)
            global_best_a = a2.copy()

    # Phase 4: micro polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        alloc = min(350.0, max(0.0, rem - 15.0))
        a3, sc3 = dense_u_run(best_n_a, n_main, alloc, 600.0, 0.01, rng, "micro")
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_n_score:
            best_n_score = float(sc3)
            best_n_a = a3.copy()
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = float(sc3)
            global_best_a = a3.copy()

    # If still above a conservative target and time remains, try a neighboring
    # length to avoid being locked into a local basin of the main length.
    if np.isfinite(global_best_score) and global_best_score > 1.5025:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 200.0:
            if n_main <= 8192:
                n_try = 16384
            else:
                n_try = 8192
            a_try = resample_to_length(best_n_a, n_try)
            alloc = min(300.0, max(0.0, rem - 120.0))
            a4, sc4 = dense_u_run(a_try, n_try, alloc, 300.0, 0.02, rng, "alt_n%d" % n_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = float(sc4)
                global_best_a = a4.copy()

    # If the previous construction is the global best, run it a bit at its own
    # length if practical and time remains.
    if prev_a is not None and prev_a.size != (global_best_a.size if global_best_a is not None else -1):
        if np.isfinite(global_best_score) and global_best_score > 1.5035:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 180.0:
                n_prev = int(prev_a.size)
                if 2048 <= n_prev <= 32768:
                    alloc = min(260.0, max(0.0, rem - 100.0))
                    a5, sc5 = dense_u_run(prev_a, n_prev, alloc, 250.0, 0.03, rng, "prev_len%d" % n_prev)
                    if np.isfinite(sc5) and sc5 < global_best_score:
                        global_best_score = float(sc5)
                        global_best_a = a5.copy()

    # Final fallback.
    if global_best_a is None or global_best_a.size == 0:
        if best_n_a is not None and best_n_a.size > 0:
            global_best_a = best_n_a
        elif prev_a is not None:
            global_best_a = prev_a
        else:
            global_best_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    global_best_a = np.asarray(global_best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    global_best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(global_best_a), global_best_a, 0.0)
    global_best_a = np.clip(global_best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s_final = float(np.sum(global_best_a))
    if s_final < 0.01:
        global_best_a = np.full(256, 1.0 / 256.0, dtype=np.float64)

    # Make sure the sum is not pathologically close to zero after any clipping.
    if float(np.sum(global_best_a)) < 0.01:
        global_best_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    # Final internal report (not using evaluate_sequence, to keep returned
    # runtime deterministic and inside the budget).
    try:
        final_report = internal_score(global_best_a)
    except Exception:
        final_report = float("nan")
    print("final length=%d internal_score=%.6f" % (global_best_a.size, final_report), flush=True)

    # Return as plain Python floats.
    return [float(x) for x in global_best_a]
```
